Method: 通过掩码持续预训练将合成个体的PII注入到OLMo（1B和7B）模型的预定义参数中，构建具有参数级真值的LACUNA测试平台。利用该平台评估当前SOTA遗忘方法的参数定位精度，并检验其对复现攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法虽在输出层面表现优异，但参数定位高度不精确，易受复现攻击。当定位成功时，即使是简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强擦除效果并对复现攻击保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位是实现有效遗忘的关键，LACUNA作为首个参数级定位基准可补充现有行为评估，推动基于定位的鲁棒遗忘方法发展。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [ExPerT: Personalizing LLM Responses to Users' Domain Expertise via Query-Wise Semantic and Keystroke Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01242)
*Yeji Park,Jiwon Tark,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ExPerT是一个查询级个性化框架，通过结合语义和击键行为线索推断用户领域专长，动态调整LLM响应的细节、术语和复杂度，将专长推断误差降低65.7%，响应满意度提升17.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态画像或纯文本信号的个性化方法无法捕捉用户查询级别的专长差异，导致LLM响应缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出ExPerT框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 语义-行为专长推断模块，通过上下文LLM提示联合解析查询文本和击键动态；(ii) 专长条件响应生成，根据推断的专长水平调整详细程度、术语和概念复杂度。

Result: 40名参与者、1270个查询的用户研究表明：专长推断误差降低65.7%（MAE 0.398 vs 1.162），响应满意度提升17.52%（5点Likert量表从3.71提升至4.36）。

Conclusion: 结合语义和行为线索的查询级专长推断能显著提升LLM个性化响应的准确性和用户满意度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by end users, yet existing personalization methods relying on static profiles or text-only signals fail to capture query-specific expertise variation. We present ExPerT, a query-wise personalization framework that adapts LLM responses to users' query domain expertise by combining semantic and behavioral cues. ExPerT consists of two key components: (i) a semantic-behavioral expertise inference module that jointly interprets query text and keystroke dynamics via in-context LLM prompting, and (ii) an expertise-conditioned response generation that adapts the level of detail, terminology, and conceptual complexity. Our user study with 40 participants and 1270 queries demonstrated that ExPerT reduced expertise inference error by 65.7% compared to the strongest baseline (MAE = 0.398 vs. 1.162) and improved response satisfaction by 17.52% (from 3.71 to 4.36) on a 5-point Likert scale.

</details>


### [184] [Mitigating Confirmation Bias through Hand-Drawing Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01359)
*Chenyu Lin,Cindy Xiong,Icy Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过展示可视化图表的动态手绘过程（而非静态图表）是否能减轻确认偏误、提高数据解读准确性。实验表明，手绘视频帮助参与者更准确地解读数据，减少信念一致性错误，增加信念超越反应，即使数据与个人先验信念相冲突时也有效。该发现为设计去偏数据界面提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化解读常受到先验信念的扭曲（确认偏误），如何减轻这种偏误、提高解读准确性是信息可视化领域的重要问题。本研究探索了一种具身化的教学方法——让观众观看可视化图表被手绘生成的过程——是否能够改善数据推理。

Method: 实验比较静态柱状图与手绘生成过程的视频，设置与参与者先验信念一致或冲突的数据情境，测量解读准确性、信念一致性错误和信念超越响应。

Result: 手绘视频帮助参与者更准确地解读数据（即使数据与先验信念冲突），减少了信念一致性错误，增加了信念超越响应。

Conclusion: 暴露可视化图表的构建过程能支持更准确的数据推理，减轻确认偏误的影响，为设计去偏数据界面开辟了有前景的设计空间。

Abstract: Understanding data visualizations is essential for informed decision-making, yet interpretation is often shaped and even distorted by prior beliefs. We investigate whether an embodied pedagogical approach, in which viewers observe the dynamic hand-drawing of a visualization, can mitigate confirmation bias and improve interpretation accuracy. We conducted a study comparing static bar charts to videos in which charts are constructed through hand-drawing, across contexts that either align with or challenge participants' prior beliefs. The results indicate that hand-drawn videos helped participants accurately interpret data, even when the data conflicted with their prior beliefs. This approach also reduced belief-consistent errors and increased belief-overriding responses. These findings suggest that exposing the construction process of a visualization supports more accurate reasoning and mitigates the influence of confirmation bias. Consequently, this work introduces a promising design space for bias-mitigating data interfaces.

</details>


### [185] [Mind the Trust Gap: Identifying (Mis)alignments in Teacher-Student Views Toward Control and Agency in K-12 Classroom AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01506)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Lisa Siegrist,Niklas Scholz,Shintaro Sato,Martina Vincoli,Man Su*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对德国16名学生和15名教师进行故事板速配研究，揭示了K-12课堂中学生与教师在AI决策控制权方面的观点错位，特别是在AI信任度、学生社交情感学习以及师生关系对AI交互的影响上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未能充分揭示教师与学生观点在K-12课堂中是否一致，尤其是涉及多利益相关者的AI课堂环境。

Method: 采用故事板速配研究方法，对16名K-12学生和15名教师进行显式配对匹配分析。

Result: 学生与教师在AI信任度、学生社交情感学习等关键主题上存在观点错位；师生关系（超出AI使用范畴）显著影响利益相关者的观点与交互。

Conclusion: 师生-AI交互中的偏好具有复杂性，取决于利益相关者之间的动态关系；需弥合视角差距以优化课堂AI应用。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based technologies have been integrated into school classrooms where multiple stakeholders (with different roles) interact with each other, it is critical to deeply understand stakeholder views in the classroom. In particular, prior work has not fully uncovered how teachers' and school students' views might or might not align well with each other, especially in K-12 classrooms. We conducted a speed-dating study using storyboards with 16 school students and 15 school teachers in Germany to investigate alignments and misalignments between their views on student-AI decision-making control in K-12 classroom. Through an explicit pair-matching analysis, we found that students and teachers had misaligned views on several key topics, including how much they trust AI and social and emotional aspects of student learning with AI. Findings also revealed the importance of teacher-student relationships outside of AI use that shape stakeholders' views and interactions. We discuss potential reasons for the observed misaligned views and strategies to fill the perspective gaps. This study illustrates the complexities of preferences in teacher-student-AI interactions that depend on the dynamic relations among the stakeholders.

</details>


### [186] [OrchestrXR: A Multi-Agent System for Idea-to-Prototype XR Study Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01588)
*Shuqi Liao,Chenfei Zhu,Karthik Ramani,Voicu Popescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OrchestrXR 是一个多智能体人机协作工作流，用于将早期 XR 研究想法转化为可运行的 Unity 原型，支持可控的研究设计、场景生成和交互生成，用户研究显示其能有效支持早期 XR 研究创作并保持用户意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将初始的 XR 研究想法转化为可运行的原型过程中，研究设计、场景构建和交互实现之间存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的端到端支持工具。

Method: 提出 OrchestrXR，一个多智能体人机协作工作流，通过结构化模式、多智能体编排和交互式人机界面，将研究想法逐步转化为基于 Unity 的原型，涵盖研究设计、场景生成和交互生成三个阶段。

Result: 12 名 XR 研究者的用户研究表明，OrchestrXR 能有效支持早期 XR 研究创作，并在各阶段保持良好的用户意图保留。

Conclusion: OrchestrXR 为早期 XR 研究创作提供了一种可控的、多阶段的端到端解决方案，弥合了从想法到原型之间的碎片化缺口。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) has become an important interaction paradigm in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI). XR studies are used to investigate interaction, perception, and user behavior in immersive environments, and typically involve experimental tasks, 3D scenes, and interactive logic. However, turning an initial XR study idea into a runnable prototype remains fragmented across study design, scene construction, and interaction implementation. We present OrchestrXR, a multi-agent human-AI workflow for early-stage idea-to-prototype XR study authoring. Rather than treating XR study creation as one-shot generation, OrchestrXR supports a controllable workflow across study design, scene generation, and interaction generation through structured schemas, multi-agent orchestration, and interactive human-agent interfaces, producing a Unity-based prototype from a researcher's idea. A user study with 12 XR researchers suggests that OrchestrXR provides effective support for early-stage XR study authoring with strong intent preservation across stages.

</details>


### [187] [Made to Feel: How Designers Bring Emotions into Affective Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01593)
*Yixin Bai,Ziyi Wang,Keke Wu,Fumeng Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对15位可视化从业者的半结构化访谈，研究了设计师如何将情感融入可视化作品中，识别出情感的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）、设计师处理情感的三个层面（数据、设计、受众）及其策略、伦理考量，并发现情感意图常在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响源于累积的设计选择而非孤立的视觉元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感可视化在研究中日益受到关注，但设计师如何在实际工作中将情感融入可视化作品这一过程尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对15位可视化从业者进行半结构化访谈，采用混合主题分析方法对访谈数据进行分析。

Result: 识别出情感的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）；设计师处理情感的三个层面（数据、设计、受众）及相关设计策略；设计过程中的伦理考量。此外发现情感意图常在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响源于累积的设计选择而非孤立的视觉元素。

Conclusion: 情感可视化设计是一个涌现性过程，情感影响由设计选择的累积效应产生，评估是实践者面临的关键挑战。

Abstract: Affective visualization is increasingly studied in visualization research, yet how designers bring emotions into their visualization work remains unexplored. This paper addresses this gap through semi-structured interviews with 15 visualization practitioners. Using hybrid thematic analysis, we identify: (1) three functions that emotions can serve for viewers (entry, engagement, outcome); (2) three facets of how designers work with emotion (data, design, audience), along with design strategies; and (3) ethical considerations in the design process. We also observe that affective intent often emerges during the design process rather than being planned from the outset, and that emotional impact arises from accumulated design choices rather than isolated visual elements. Finally, we highlight evaluation as a key challenge identified by our participants.

</details>


### [188] [Evaluating Glanceable Multi-Device Family Health Tracking with Smartwatches and Home Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01618)
*Lucas M. Silva,Evropi Stefanidi,Aehong Min,Franceli L. Cibrian,Jesus A. Beltran,Cassie Zeiler,Sabrina E. B. Schuck,Kimberley D. Lakes,Gillian R. Hayes,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过9周的实地实验，对比了智能手表、家庭显示屏以及两者组合三种设计对家庭情绪与目标追踪行为的影响。发现组合设计（多设备冗余）能显著提升追踪频率，适应不同家庭成员在作息、移动性和设备偏好上的多样性，并支持非参与者的间接参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泛在计算研究多关注个人健康追踪设备，但对面向家庭这一协作单元的多设备健康信息学设计理解不足。家庭健康管理具有天然协作性，需要探讨不同设备配置如何影响家庭成员的追踪行为与协作感知。

Method: 招募12个家庭共44人，在9周内交替使用三种设计：仅智能手表、仅家庭显示屏、两者组合。通过日志数据分析追踪频率、时间分布与地点分布，结合访谈分析用户感知。

Result: 1）家庭显示屏存在时，情绪与目标追踪频率显著高于仅用智能手表；2）整体情绪追踪随时间下降；3）追踪在下午达到峰值、周末下降，家中追踪频率是户外的2.6倍；4）儿童追踪一致性高于成人；5）智能手表支持外出时的机会性追踪与远程感知，家庭显示屏提醒在家成员自追踪并促进协作，包括非参与者（如未佩戴设备的成员）。

Conclusion: 多设备冗余设计能包容家庭成员在日程、移动性和设备偏好上的差异，建议未来家庭健康信息学应朝包容性、可一瞥式查看和适应性数据共享方向发展。

Abstract: While ubiquitous computing research has explored diverse devices for personal health tracking, we know less about multi-device designs for family informatics, where health management is inherently collaborative. To understand how families adopt and perceive ubiquitous access to shared health data across contexts, we evaluated smartwatch-only, home display-only, and combined designs for tracking moods and goals, domains central to family health behavior regulation. 44 people across 12 families alternated between these designs over nine weeks. Log analysis revealed that mood tracking and goal reporting were significantly more frequent with the home display present compared to smartwatch-only use, despite an overall decline in mood tracking over time. Tracking peaked in afternoons, dropped on weekends, and occurred 2.6X more at home, with children tracking more consistently than adults across all designs. From interview analysis, we learned how family data glanceability on smartwatches supported opportunistic tracking and awareness while apart, whereas displays reminded families to self-track and collaborate during home routines including members that avoided wearables (e.g., non-participants). Multi-device redundancy accommodated diversity in routines, mobility patterns, and device preferences among members in the same family. We discuss opportunities for multi-device family informatics that accommodates different preferences through inclusive, glanceable, and adaptable ubiquitous data sharing.

</details>


### [189] [From Answer Generators to Reasoning Facilitators: Designing AI Tutors for Mathematical Reasoning in High-Stakes Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01692)
*Yuming Feng,Yuan Tian,Erica Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了AITutor系统，通过将理论教学机制转化为具体UI功能，在初中生中考备考场景中部署，研究发现学生在时间压力下会主动规避传统的苏格拉底式对话，转而使用"答案优先"的捷径作为诊断检查点；通过分层示例、步骤链接的视觉锚定和元认知支架等功能降低推理修复的交互成本，最终贡献了"以推理为中心的产品循环"设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育技术中的快速集成威胁将数学学习简化为单纯的答案生成，亟需设计能够真正支持数学推理的AI交互系统。

Method: 生成性研究、可用性研究以及12名初中生参与的真实环境部署，通过混合方法三角验证（7,379个遥测事件、8次情境观察、10次访谈）收集数据。

Result: 学生在时间压力下会主动规避传统苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"的捷径重新用作重要的诊断检查点；分层示例、步骤链接的视觉锚定和元认知支架等功能有效降低了推理修复的交互成本。

Conclusion: 贡献了"以推理为中心的产品循环"，为设计能够在真实环境中结构化支持数学推理的检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI系统提供了可行启示。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into educational technology threatens to reduce mathematical learning to mere answer generation. This paper presents a generative study, usability study, and 12-participant field deployment of AITutor, an interactive system that translates theoretical pedagogical mechanisms into concrete user interface features. We explore how junior-high students preparing for high-stakes exams (Zhongkao) interact with AI tutoring. Through mixed-methods triangulation (7,379 telemetry events, 8 contextual observations, 10 interviews), we reveal that students actively resist traditional Socratic dialogue under time pressure, repurposing "answer-first" shortcuts as vital diagnostic checkpoints. We demonstrate how features like layered worked examples, step-linked visual grounding, and metacognitive scaffolding lower the interaction cost of reasoning repair. We contribute a "Reasoning-Centered Product Loop," offering actionable implications for designing AI that structurally supports the inspection, local repair, curriculum verification, and delayed retrieval of mathematical reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [190] [Adapting CCDF Plots for Visualizing Ordinal Regression Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01747)
*Abhraneel Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用修改后的互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化累积链接序数回归模型的结果，以解决序数回归模型在可视化领域的采用率低的问题，并展示其如何直观传达原本需要将序数数据当作度量数据处理才能得到的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序数回归模型相比线性模型或ANOVA对序数数据（如李克特量表）的假设更宽松，但在可视化及相关领域采用率低。作者推测原因之一是该模型的结果难以直观可视化和传达关键发现。

Method: 提出使用（修改后的）互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化累积链接序数回归模型的结果。

Result: 通过mCCDF图，可以轻松传达那些原本需要将序数数据当作度量数据（如线性模型/ANOVA）处理才能呈现的研究结论。

Conclusion: mCCDF图能够有效解决序数回归模型结果可视化困难的问题，有望促进该模型在相关领域的采用。

Abstract: Cumulative-link ordinal regression models are an alternative approach for analysing ordinal data such as Likert items, which are widely used in Visualization (and other related fields like HCI, psychology etc.). There are many researchers who are strong proponents of this approach, as it makes less stringent assumptions about the data, compared to the more commonly used linear model or ANOVA. Yet, ordinal regression models have seen limited adoption. I posit that one possible reason for this might be due to the difficulty in visually representing the results from such models, and in communicating the key takeaways in an intuitive manner. I propose the use of (modified) Complementary Cumulative Distribution Function (mCCDF) plots to visualize the results of ordinal regression models, and demonstrate how the same takeaways that researchers present from analyses which treat ordinal data as metric can be easily communicated using mCCDFs.

</details>


### [191] [A Social Norms Approach to Youth Social Media Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01807)
*JaeWon Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该博士论文提出，年轻人在社交媒体上难以实现真实的自我表达、减少评判和建立信任，其障碍并非个人选择，而是平台规范、同伴感知和从众心理等社会规范因素。论文围绕三个核心论点展开：平台规范导致多元无知（青年执行自己私下排斥的规范）；设计干预受现有规范影响，隐私等关系目标应作为社会规范而非个人设置；社会对“社交媒体”等同于主流平台的认知限制了政策与设计创新。研究最终导向直接让青年参与设计并构建一个以建立可信连接为明确目标的独立平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人一致表示希望在社交媒体上实现真实的自我表达、减少评判和增加人际信任，但实际上很少能做到。现有研究多归因于个人选择，但论文认为真正的障碍是社会规范层面的，而非个体的意志力或技能问题。

Method: 采用社会规范方法，将青年社交媒体设计置于规范框架下分析；通过三个理论主张逐层推演，最终以参与式设计方法直接让青年参与构建一个基于证据的独立平台。

Result: 识别出三种规范性障碍：平台规范产生的多元无知现象；现有规范对设计干预的约束作用；以及主流平台定义社会媒体概念对政策与设计创新的限制。

Conclusion: 应从社会规范而非个人选择的视角理解青年在社交媒体上的行为困境；设计的核心不是修补主流平台，而是构建一个从信号层面就明确传递“建立可信连接”这一目标的新型平台，并让青年直接参与平台的设计与建设过程。

Abstract: Young people consistently say they want authentic self-expression, less judgment, and more interpersonal trust on social media, yet they rarely manage to engage that way. My dissertation argues that the obstacle is normative rather than individual: how youth engage is governed less by personal choice than by platform norms, peer perception, and beliefs about how others behave. I take a social norms approach to youth social media design organized around three claims. First, platform norms constrain individual behavior, producing a pluralistic ignorance in which youth enact norms they privately reject. Second, design interventions are themselves shaped by existing norms, so whether a feature works depends on the environment around it, which means relational goals such as privacy must be treated as social norms rather than individual settings. Third, a societal norm about what ``social media'' is -- equating it with a few mainstream platforms -- confines policy and design to mitigating those platforms rather than actively envisioning supportive alternatives. Together these claims motivate my dissertation research: engaging youth directly in designing and building an evidence-based independent platform whose features consistently signal that building trusted connections is what the space is for.

</details>


### [192] [Visual Analytics of Neighborhood Attribute Profiles for Exploring Structural Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02163)
*Kohei Arimoto,Masahiko Itoh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过降维可视化方法分析属性网络中节点的高维邻域特征空间，发现结构角色形成具有密度偏差的非线性流形，揭示了行业分类与网络拓扑结构之间的不一致性，指出相同语义标签可能对应不同的结构角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法隐式假设特征空间是均匀且连续的，但真实网络的高维特征空间拓扑结构复杂，需要可视化分析方法来揭示其真实结构。

Method: 采用降维可视化方法分析节点邻域属性特征形成的高维特征空间，以企业间交易网络为案例进行研究。

Result: (1) 供应链层级连续过渡；(2) 语义相同的类别在实际交易网络中可被清晰分离；(3) 单一行业标签可能分裂为多个区域。

Conclusion: 相同的语义标签不一定对应相似的结构角色，未来需要开发与流形拓扑对齐的新的相似性度量方法。

Abstract: Exploring similar nodes in attributed networks represents a key challenge in data mining. While recent representation learning methods embed networks into low-dimensional vectors, they often implicitly assume a uniform and continuous feature space. This paper proposes a visual analytics approach using dimensionality reduction to help clarify the true topological structure of high-dimensional feature spaces formed by nodes' neighborhood attribute profiles. Analyzing inter-firm transaction networks indicates that structural roles can form complex, non-linear manifolds with density biases. Comparing this feature space with industry classifications suggested: (1) supply chain hierarchies transition continuously; (2) categories treated identically under general semantics can be clearly separated by actual transaction networks; and (3) a single industry label may fragment into multiple regions. These findings suggest potential limitations in assuming identical semantics imply similar structural roles and highlight the possible need for new similarity metrics aligned with manifold topology.

</details>


### [193] [Synthetic Contact with AI Reduces Cross-Partisan Animosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02181)
*Benjamin Lira,Noah Castelo,Stefano Puntoni,Olivier Toubia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过五项预注册研究（总N=3,960）证明，与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话（合成接触）可以有效降低接触门槛、纠正误解、改善情感态度，并促使人们更愿意与真实对立党派成员交流，尽管效果在一周后部分消退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国两党之间的情感温暖度在过去三十年急剧下降，但有意义的跨党派接触仍然稀缺，因为人们主动回避这种接触。研究者希望探索AI聊天机器人能否替代人们回避的真实跨党派接触。

Method: 五项预注册实验，总样本量3,960名美国党派人士。包括：1）测量回避意愿（用死亡思考作为对照）；2）单次10分钟对话的前后测；3）三臂实验排除纯参与和社交性作为驱动因素；4）行为层面测试（是否愿意与真实对立党派对话）；5）一周后追踪效果持久性，并分析对话内容。

Result: 1）人们宁愿思考死亡近两倍的时间来避免与真人对话，而非AI；2）单次10分钟对话纠正了错误认知并改善了情感态度；3）排除纯参与和社交性假说；4）对话后参与者有真实对话意愿提升6个百分点；5）情感改善效果在一周后大部分消退，但在最极端的党派人士中留有微小残余；6）信息内容（而非友好度）是外群体AI聊天机器人区别于对照组的关键。

Conclusion: 合成接触是一种可规模化、行为上有意义且被广泛接受的跨党派接触形式，尽管其效果持久性有限，但为缓解政治极化提供了新的干预途径。

Abstract: Americans' warmth toward members of the opposing political party has fallen sharply over the past three decades -- yet meaningful cross-partisan contact remains scarce, in part because people actively avoid it. Across five preregistered studies (total N = 3,960 U.S. partisans), we test whether brief conversations with AI chatbots representing the political outgroup can substitute for the contact people shun. Synthetic contact first lowers the barrier to entry: partisans would endure almost twice as long contemplating their own mortality to avoid a human outgroup partner as an AI one. These conversations then correct the misperceptions that fuel division. At baseline, Democrats placed Republicans more than a standard deviation past their actual position on environmental consumption attitudes -- enough to flip the average Republican from supportive to opposed -- and a single ten-minute conversation with an outgroup chatbot corrected those beliefs and warmed affect in a within-person study of both parties. A three-arm experiment ruled out pure engagement and sociality as drivers. Synthetic contact also moved behavior, in a sample of both parties and on a more affectively charged issue: participants who spoke with an outgroup bot about immigration were six percentage points more likely than controls to choose to have a real conversation with a partisan from the other side. A final study tested whether these gains last: the warmth effect replicated immediately in a new sample; most of it faded within a week, with a small residual concentrated among the most extreme partisans. Analyzing conversation content showed that information, more than friendliness, distinguishes outgroup bots from control chatbots. Together, these findings establish synthetic contact as a scalable, behaviorally consequential, and -- unlike face-to-face contact -- widely acceptable form of cross-partisan engagement.

</details>


### [194] [What Types of Human-AI Teams Exist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02198)
*Nathan Hughes,Ibrahim Habli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分析了53篇人机协作团队研究论文，基于心理学团队分类法将其划分为五类：AI助手、临时依赖、强制临时依赖、配对平等和群体平等。研究发现多种不同团队类型被归在同一概念下，质疑结论的可迁移性，并提供了识别团队类型、研究报告清单及领域整合建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作团队研究在多领域广泛开展，但缺乏统一的分类框架来理解不同类型的团队及其异同，导致研究结论难以比较和迁移。

Method: 对53篇人机协作团队论文进行系统分析，基于心理学团队分类法将其聚类为五种类型。

Result: 识别出五类人机协作团队（AI助手、临时依赖、强制临时依赖、配对平等、群体平等），证明现有研究将多种异质团队类型归入同一"人机协作团队"定义下。

Conclusion: 不同类型的人机协作团队之间结论可迁移性存疑；提供了识别团队类型的指南、研究报告清单及领域整合建议。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming has received increasing attention in the literature. However, the range of studies conducted in multiple domains make it difficult to understand what types of teams are being studied, and in what ways are they similar/different from one another. In this study, we analyse 53 papers on human-AI teams and categorise them into five main clusters based on psychological taxonomies of teaming; AI Assistant, Ad-hoc Dependency, Ad-hoc Forced Dependency, Paired Equanimity, and Group Equanimity. Each cluster represents a unique combination of holistic team-level characteristics, indicating there are multiple disparate team types studied under the same definition. In turn, this raises the question of whether insights are truly transferable between papers. We conclude with guidance on how to identify the types of human-AI teams studied, a checklist for reporting a human-AI team in research work, and ways in which the field can be further synthesised.

</details>


### [195] [Personality Without Persons? A Psychometric Critique of Big Five Testing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02325)
*Kim Zierahn,Cristina Cachero,Anna Korhonen,Nuria Oliver*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大五人格测量在LLMs中的心理测量学有效性，发现：1）为LLM改编的项目可达足够内容效度，但原始人类项目不行；2）大五量表无法捕捉模型间的有意义差异（仅解释3%方差）；3）LLM响应无法恢复大五五因子结构（4个因子塌缩为一个）；指令微调使得分向社会期望偏移。结论：大五人格框架不适用于LLM，需开发专门评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大五人格问卷越来越多被用于描述、比较和管理LLMs，但这些工具是为人类开发验证的，其适用于LLMs的效度尚未得到系统检验。作者旨在通过心理测量学评估，检验大五人格量表在LLMs中是否具有内容效度、区分效度和结构效度。

Method: 1）评估5种候选大五量表的内容效度；2）将最优量表施测于N=244个不同模型（覆盖49个模型家族）；3）分析模型间变异（方差分解）；4）探索性因子分析检验五因子结构；5）对比基础模型与指令微调模型的大五得分差异。

Result: 1）仅经LLM改编的项目达到足够内容效度；2）模型间变异极小，仅占总得分方差的3%；3）LLM响应未恢复五因子结构，4个因子高度相关（r≥0.92）并塌缩为一个因子；4）指令微调使大五得分系统性地向社会期望特征偏移。

Conclusion: 大五人格得分在LLMs中测量的是与人类人格不等价的构念。将人类人格框架应用于LLM会产生误导性的表征，用于基准测试、比较和治理LLM可能带来问题。亟需为LLM开发专门的评估框架，而非未经验证地借用人类构念。

Abstract: Human personality inventories are increasingly used to characterize large language models (LLMs), compare systems, and inform downstream governance claims. Yet, these inventories were developed and validated for humans, and it remains unclear whether they apply to LLMs. We present a systematic psychometric evaluation of Big Five personality measurements in LLMs. We ask three research questions: Do Big Five inventories a) appropriately describe LLMs, b) capture inter-individual differences across models, and c) reflect internal factors consistent with human personality. We assess content validity of five candidate Big Five inventories and administer the winning inventory to N = 244 different models spanning 49 model families. First, we found that Big Five items adapted for LLMs can reach sufficient content validity, while original human-developed items did not. Second, Big Five inventories did not capture meaningful differences between LLMs: We found low variability between models, accounting for only 3% of total score variance. Third, LLMs responses did not recover the Big Five five-factor structure with four of the Big Five facets collapsing into one (r >= .92). Direct comparisons between base and instruction-tuned model variants suggested that alignment training systematically shifted Big Five scores toward socially desirable traits. These findings demonstrate that Big Five scores do not measure a construct equivalent to human personality in LLMs. Applying human personality frameworks to LLMs produces misleading characterizations used to benchmark, compare, and govern LLMs. We highlight the need for evaluation frameworks that are developed for LLMs, rather than adopting human constructs without validation.

</details>


### [196] [Data Comics for Education: Evaluating Effectiveness, Benefits, and the Ethics of AI-Assisted Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02361)
*Zirui Shan,Vanessa Echeverria,Yuheng Li,Yi-Shan Tsai,Roberto Martinez-Maldonado*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过60名大学生的被试内实验，发现使用GenAI辅助生成的数据漫画相比传统可视化图表，能显著提升学生在信息检索和理解任务中的表现，尤其在高阶洞察理解任务中效果更佳，且不受学生先验可视化素养影响。学生认为数据漫画更具吸引力和易理解性，但也关注AI生成内容的误导风险和所有权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在解读数据可视化方面存在可视化素养不足的问题，数据漫画作为新兴媒介虽有望提升参与度和理解力，但其教育价值缺乏实证检验，且创作成本高。GenAI的进步为解决创作瓶颈提供了可扩展的方案。

Method: 采用被试内设计，招募60名大学生，对比传统可视化图表与借助GenAI工具生成的数据漫画在信息检索和理解任务上的效果，同时测量学生的先验可视化素养水平。

Result: 学生在数据漫画条件下表现 consistently 更好，尤其在洞察理解任务中优势显著，且效果独立于先验可视化素养。学生主观评价数据漫画更具吸引力和易理解性，但同时对GenAI驱动的错误信息和所有权问题表达担忧。

Conclusion: 数据漫画有望成为教育中有效的数据沟通工具，但需要关注AI辅助创作带来的伦理问题（如误导信息和所有权）。

Abstract: In today's data-driven world, students often struggle with interpreting visualisations due to limited visualisation literacy. Data comics have emerged as a promising medium to enhance engagement and understanding, but their educational value has seen little empirical examination, partly due to the effort required to create them. Recent advances in Generative AI (GenAI) offer a scalable solution to this challenge. We conducted a within-subjects study with 60 university students, comparing conventional visualisations with data comics, created with assistance from GenAI tools, across information retrieval and comprehension tasks. Students consistently performed better with data comics, particularly in insight comprehension tasks, independent of prior visualisation literacy. Students also commented data comics as more engaging and easier to understand, though concerns were raised about GenAI-driven misinformation and ownership. Our findings highlight the potential of data comics as a potentially effective tool for data communication in education, while underscoring the need to address ethical concerns related to AI-assisted creation.

</details>


### [197] [Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02430)
*Wen Ying,Seongkook Heo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究对比了无触觉反馈、触觉反馈（振动与压力）和物理表面三种手部接地触觉反馈方式对VR高精度触摸任务（选择、追踪、草图绘制）的影响。结果发现，便携式物理表面在选择精度、追踪效率和草图质量上表现最优，并增加了双手协同使用率，用户更偏好物理表面带来的控制感和信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统中空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参考面，导致高精度触摸交互困难，因此需要探究不同手部接地触觉反馈对VR触摸任务性能的影响。

Method: 设计了三种触觉反馈条件：无触觉反馈（仅视觉）、触觉反馈（振动+压力）、物理表面（便携式可触摸表面），通过选择、追踪和草图绘制三类高精度任务评估性能。

Result: 物理表面在任务执行精度、效率和质量上均优于其他条件，且提升了双手协作使用率；用户偏好物理表面，归因于更强的控制感和信心。

Conclusion: 为VR高精度交互提供物理表面触觉接地可显著提升任务表现和用户体验，物理表面是优于纯触觉反馈的交互方案。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface.
  Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.

</details>


### [198] [When Do LLM Personas Support Visualization Design? A Cross-Model Study of Color Assignment and Chart Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02455)
*Shahreen Salim,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM人格角色在可视化设计中的表现高度依赖模型选择、任务框架和概念类型，人格-颜色关联在不同模型中表现不一致，图表偏好主要受任务上下文驱动而非人格特质，因此LLM人格角色仅可作为探索性工具而非人类替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM人格角色在可视化设计任务中反映的是稳定的人格效应，还是模型选择和任务框架的人为产物。

Method: 在颜色分配（抽象/具体概念）和图表偏好两个可视化任务中，使用43个Big Five人格剖面，在GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1-mini和GPT-5-mini三个模型上进行系统比较。

Result: 人格-颜色耦合高度依赖模型配置：GPT-4o-mini中完全缺失，GPT-4.1-mini中一致存在，GPT-5-mini中部分存在；抽象概念中人格解释的色调方差多于模型身份；图表偏好中无人格基线在8/9的模型-上下文组合中恢复相同的top-1选择。

Conclusion: LLM人格角色可作为可视化设计的探索性探针，但不能替代人类被试，未来研究应进行多模型测试、概念类型分解和设置无人格基线。

Abstract: Large language model personas are increasingly used to approximate diverse users during early-stage visualization design, but it remains unclear whether persona-conditioned outputs reflect stable personality effects or artifacts of model choice and task framing. We examine this question across two visualization-relevant tasks: color assignment for abstract and concrete concepts, and chart-idiom preference ratings across task contexts. Using 43 Big Five profiles across GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4.1-mini, and GPT-5-mini, we find that personality-color coupling is highly model-configuration dependent: absent in GPT-4o-mini for all six concepts, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini across all six, and partial in GPT-5-mini for two of six. Concept type further shapes the signal: for abstract concepts, personality explains more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts show smaller and comparable effects. In chart choice, trait-aligned cluster aggregation produces stable top-idiom rankings across all nine cluster-context combinations, but a no-persona baseline recovers the same top choice in 8 of 9 model-context cells, indicating that task context drives rank-1 selection more than personality. These findings position LLM personas as exploratory probes for visualization design, not substitutes for human participants, and motivate multi-model testing, concept-type disaggregation, and no-persona baselines in future studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [WaveLander: A Generalizable Hierarchical Control Framework for UAV Landing on Wave-Disturbed Platforms via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01281)
*Chun-Kit Li,Iok Long Sit,Ming Fung Siu,Ka Yu Kui,Hin Wang Lin,Pengyu Wang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WaveLander，一种通过强化学习实现的分层控制框架，将无人机在波浪扰动海洋平台上的自主着陆问题分解为高层垂直着陆决策（RL策略输出标量垂直速度参考）和低层飞行稳定控制（传统控制器），在随机波浪运动仿真中展现出鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在波浪扰动海洋平台上的自主着陆因平台随机运动、姿态时变和触地条件不确定而极具挑战性；现有基于模型的方法需要精确运动预测和在线优化，端到端学习方法训练复杂度高且可解释性有限。

Method: 提出WaveLander分层控制框架：高层采用强化学习策略，将平台相对观测映射为标量垂直速度参考；底层使用传统飞行控制器维持姿态稳定和横向跟踪。该框架将动态平台着陆简化为低维、时序感知的控制问题，无需显式切换规则即可实现平滑着陆。

Result: 在随机波浪激励的平台运动仿真下，WaveLander实现了鲁棒的着陆性能，并能泛化到未见过的扰动条件。

Conclusion: 分层学习控制方法在海洋无人机回收任务中展现出潜力，有效结合了强化学习的决策能力和传统控制的稳定性。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) on wave-disturbed marine platforms remains challenging due to stochastic platform motion, time-varying platform attitude, and uncertain touchdown conditions. Existing model-based methods often require accurate motion prediction and online optimization, while end-to-end learning approaches may suffer from high training complexity and limited interpretability. This paper presents WaveLander, a hierarchical control framework via reinforcement learning (RL) that decouples vertical landing decision-making from low-level flight stabilization. The RL policy maps a compact platform-relative observation to a scalar vertical velocity reference, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains attitude stability and lateral tracking. This formulation reduces dynamic platform landing to a low-dimensional, timing-aware control problem and enables smooth landing behavior without explicit switching rules. Simulation results under randomized wave-induced platform motions show that WaveLander achieves robust landing performance and generalizes to unseen disturbance conditions, demonstrating the potential of hierarchical learning-based control for marine UAV recovery.

</details>


### [200] [Adaptive Companionship for Group-Following Robots: Handling Dynamically Changing Group Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01287)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的社会性机器人自适应群体伴随方法，通过语义推理推断伴行位置、维持社交距离并理解群体动态，结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器保证稳定性与安全性。实验表明该方法在成功率和碰撞率上分别提升15%和降低25%，用户研究也认为其伴行行为自然且符合社交规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类群体通常不遵循固定队形，这使得机器人难以在与人类同行时保持自然伴行行为，亟需一种能够自适应理解群体动态的伴随方法。

Method: 先检测群体成员，通过感知模块生成交互群体空间的视觉表征，输入VLM进行语义推理；然后结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器规划路径，确保稳定性和安全性。

Result: 在五个场景中，该方法相比基线方案成功提升了15%的成功率，碰撞率降低了25%；用户研究也表明生成的伴行行为自然且符合社交规范。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的语义推理能力能有效帮助机器人理解群体动态并实现自适应伴行，该方法在性能和社交接受度上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Accompanying a group of humans is an essential aspect of developing human-like social cognition in robots. However, human groups typically do not follow fixed formations, which poses significant challenges for robots in maintaining natural companionship behaviors. In this paper, we propose an adaptive group-accompaniment method for social robots based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), leveraging their semantic reasoning capabilities to infer companion positions, maintain social distances, and understand group dynamics. The members of the group are first detected, and a perceptual module generates visual representations of the interaction group space as input to the VLM, which is then combined with a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller to ensure stability and safety. Experimental evaluations across five scenarios show that the proposed method enables robots to accompany the group effectively, demonstrating a 15\% improvement in success rate and a 25\% reduction in collision rate compared to baseline approaches. Additionally, a user study indicates that the generated companionship behaviors are perceived as natural and socially appropriate.

</details>


### [201] [The Three Dimensions of ROS 2 Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01304)
*Sanghoon Lee,Taehun Kim,Angelo Corsaro,Kyung-Joon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了ROS 2中间件架构，提出了从空间、时间、状态三个结构维度分析分布式机器人中间件局限性的概念框架，揭示了在动态无线环境下三者间的结构性权衡，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROS 2中间件（DDS、Zenoh等）在动态且资源受限的无线环境中存在结构性局限，缺乏系统性分析框架来理解这些局限及其相互关联。

Method: 通过系统综述方法，提出空间-时间-状态三维概念框架，对中间件架构、发现机制、数据交换和状态管理进行结构化分析，并综述已有实现和先前研究。

Result: 揭示了三个结构性权衡：空间抽象会掩盖网络可变性并削弱时间保障；状态连续性机制引入计算和网络开销，与时间关键通信竞争；在受限无线条件下这些权衡构成当代机器人中间件的实际极限。

Conclusion: 通过综合架构模式并识别当前建模与分析方法的缺口，为构建鲁棒且可扩展的机器人中间件架构提供了原则性的研究路线图。

Abstract: ROS 2 (Robot Operating System 2) has emerged as the de facto standard for modern robot software development, with middleware implementations such as the Data Distribution Service (DDS) and Zenoh forming the core infrastructure for distributed robotic communication. Despite their architectural flexibility, these middleware systems exhibit structural limitations, particularly under dynamic and resource-constrained wireless environments. This paper presents a systematic survey of ROS 2 middleware and introduces a conceptual framework to examine its architectural limits through three structural dimensions required by distributed robotic systems, namely Space, Time, and State. We first provide a structured analysis of middleware architecture and operational dynamics, including discovery, data exchange, and state management mechanisms. Building on this foundation, we formalize Time as temporal predictability for control loops, Space as spatial abstraction from physical topology to enable modular deployment, and State as contextual continuity despite dynamic node participation and intermittent connectivity. Through a comprehensive review of existing implementations and prior studies, we organize middleware research according to the structural trade-offs that arise among these dimensions. Under constrained wireless conditions, spatial abstraction can obscure network variability and weaken temporal guarantees, while mechanisms that preserve state continuity introduce computational and network overhead that competes with time-critical communication. These interactions reveal structural trade-offs that characterize the practical limits of contemporary robot middleware. By synthesizing architectural patterns and identifying gaps in current modeling and analysis approaches, this survey outlines a principled research roadmap for robust and scalable robotic middleware architectures.

</details>


### [202] [Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models via Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01378)
*William English,Hao Zheng,Rickard Ewetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向流匹配VLA模型的神经符号安全引导机制，通过在去噪过程中求解最小范数约束优化问题来实现预测性碰撞避免，在SafeLIBERO基准上相比单步方法实现了6.3%的碰撞避免提升和19.8%的任务成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的安全措施仅能防止机器人下一步动作造成的碰撞，缺乏对轨迹级预测性碰撞的规避能力。

Method: 将安全约束形式化为最小范数约束优化问题，在流匹配VLA的迭代去噪过程中对中间轨迹预测进行安全违规校正，通过符号约束满足与神经轨迹生成的交替实现预测性碰撞避免。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准上实现82.8%碰撞避免率和81.6%任务成功率，相比单步方法分别提升6.3%和19.8%，在长期任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全引导机制能有效实现预测性碰撞避免，特别是在复合分布偏移最显著的长期任务中表现突出。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their real-world deployment remains limited by the lack of effective safety measures. Specifically, existing safety measures only prevent collisions caused by the robot's next action. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic safety guidance mechanism for flow matching based VLAs that enables predictive collision avoidance. Flow matching based VLAs determine the next actions by predicting a trajectory (a sequence of actions) through an iterative neural flow matching process. Our method formulates safety enforcement as a minimum-norm constrained optimization problem that corrects safety violations during the denoising process of noisy intermediate trajectory predictions. By analyzing predicted trajectories and applying corrections during iterative denoising, our approach anticipates collisions before they become unavoidable. This interleaving of symbolic constraint satisfaction with neural trajectory generation enables predictive collision avoidance rather than reactive intervention. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, our method achieves 82.8% collision avoidance and 81.6% task success, a 6.3% and 19.8% improvement respectively over single-step methods, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks where compounding distribution shift is most pronounced. Video demonstrations of our approach are included on our project page at https://willenglish.tech/SafetyGuidedFlowMatching/.

</details>


### [203] [CommonRoad-Game: A Human-in-the-Loop Simulation Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01382)
*Yunfei Bi,Youran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CommonRoad-Game，一个轻量级人机协同仿真框架，集成于CommonRoad平台，通过多线程架构和同步机制实现确定性时序交互，支持运动规划器测试与人类驾驶行为分析，实验表明其具有良好的时间同步性、可扩展多智能体仿真能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台要么依赖离线数据集、缺乏实时人机交互接口，要么计算开销过大，不适合自动驾驶早期研究中快速原型开发与灵活实验。

Method: 提出多线程架构与鲁棒同步机制，将仿真时间与真实时钟对齐，实现自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶车辆之间的确定性和时序一致交互；提供场景生成模块记录驾驶日志，支持构建多样且可复现的测试用例。

Result: 实验表明CommonRoad-Game实现了稳定的时间同步，支持可扩展的多智能体仿真，并能无缝集成CommonRoad兼容的运动规划器以生成交互式驾驶场景。

Conclusion: CommonRoad-Game填补了现有仿真平台在人机交互测试方面的空白，为自动驾驶早期研究提供了一个轻量级、高效的人机协同仿真工具。

Abstract: Motion planning algorithms should be evaluated in human-in-the-loop environments to ensure they produce safe and efficient behaviors during interactions. However, existing simulation platforms often rely on recorded datasets, lack dedicated interfaces for real-time human interaction, or remain weakly integrated with an autonomous driving ecosystem. Moreover, many human-in-the-loop simulators are computationally intensive by design, making them less suitable for rapid prototyping and flexible experimentation in early-stage autonomous driving research. To address these limitations, we present CommonRoad-Game, a lightweight human-in-the-loop simulation framework tightly integrated with the CommonRoad platform, focusing on the systematic testing of motion planners with human participation and the analysis of human driving behaviors in interactive scenarios. We introduce a multi-threaded architecture with a robust synchronization mechanism that aligns simulation time with wall-clock time, enabling deterministic and temporally consistent interaction between autonomous and human-driven vehicles. In addition, the framework provides a scenario generation module that records driving logs, allowing diverse and reproducible test cases to be constructed from human-in-the-loop experiments. Experimental results demonstrate that CommonRoad-Game achieves stable temporal synchronization, supports scalable multi-agent simulation, and seamlessly integrates CommonRoad-compatible motion planners to generate interactive driving scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Yunfei-Bi8/CommonRoad-Game.

</details>


### [204] [BIFROST: Bridging Invariant Feature Representation for Observation-space Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01410)
*Yunfu Deng,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIFROST提出通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享历史编码器，将产生等价长期行为的观测-动作序列映射到相近的潜在状态，从而在仿真和现实间建立共享结构，实现零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim2real方法分别处理视觉和动力学域差异，通过独立适应模块组合来应对多种差距共存的情况，但忽视了仿真与现实任务间存在共享结构这一根本前提。

Method: 提出BIFROST框架，利用配对跨域数据，通过跨域双模拟目标（cross-domain bisimulation objective）学习共享历史编码器，使产生等价长期行为的观测-动作序列在不同域中被映射到相近的潜在状态，再基于这些潜在状态在仿真中训练策略。

Result: 在sim2sim视觉导航、sim2real接触丰富操作任务和视觉伺服任务上，BIFROST实现了有效迁移，而域适应和协同训练基线在视觉和动力学域差距同时存在时均失败。

Conclusion: 通过对齐跨域共享的长期行为结构而非域间表观特征，BIFROST能够在仿真和现实同时存在视觉与动力学差异时实现零样本策略迁移。

Abstract: Sim2real transfer for robot policy learning suffers due to mismatch between simulation and reality. Existing methods typically address each gap in isolation through separate adaptation modules, which are composed or layered when both gaps coexist. Yet the basis for attempting sim2real in the first place is that there is shared structure between a task in simulation and reality, where equivalent actions from equivalent configurations produce equivalent long term outcomes regardless of domain specific differences in rendering or physics. In this paper, we study whether we can identify and exploit this shared structure from raw observations to train a policy that enables zero shot transfer. We introduce BIFROST, which learns a shared history encoder on paired cross-domain data via cross-domain bisimulation objective: observation-action sequences leading to equivalent long-term behavior are mapped to nearby latent states, regardless of domain. Policies trained on these latent states in simulation transfer zero-shot to reality. We provide empirical evidence on sim2sim visual navigation and sim2real contact rich manipulation task and visual servoing task that BIFROST achieves effective transfer where domain adaptation and co-training baselines fail under both visual and dynamics domain gaps.

</details>


### [205] [SE(2) Navigation Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01454)
*Shuyang Shi,Kaixian Qu,Changan Chen,Ines Kast,Yuntao Ma,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SE(2)导航网格，一种编码偏航相关可通行性的多边形表示方法，结合A*-路径拉直-A*分层规划策略，在复杂多层环境中实现地面机器人的高效导航，仿真中可通行区域比传统导航网格多50%以上，并在真实机器人上验证了实时在线生成与导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航表示存在关键局限：点云和占据地图缺乏显式表面结构难以评估可通行性，稠密三角网格直接寻路计算开销过大，而传统导航网格假设偏航不变的可通行性，不适用于非圆形机器人在受限空间中的导航。

Method: 提出SE(2)导航网格，使用足迹掩码评估偏航相关的可通行性，构建具有显式平移和旋转连接的多层偏航图。基于此表示，开发A*-路径拉直-A*分层路径规划策略，两级优化机器人位置和朝向。同时提出从流式点云增量更新网格的在线方法。

Result: 仿真中SE(2)导航网格比传统导航网格捕获的可通行区域多50%以上，SE(2)导航网格+ASA管线在受限环境中持续优于基于采样的基线方法。真实机器人实验验证了实时在线生成和多环境导航的成功。

Conclusion: SE(2)导航网格通过编码偏航相关的可通行性，有效弥补了传统导航网格在非圆形机器人受限空间导航中的不足，结合分层路径规划策略实现了计算高效且可在线更新的导航方案。

Abstract: Global navigation for ground robots in complex multi-level environments requires representations that accurately capture traversable regions while enabling efficient path planning. Current approaches present key limitations: Point clouds and volumetric occupancy maps lack explicit surface structure for traversability estimation, whereas direct pathfinding on dense triangle meshes is computationally prohibitive. Navigation meshes mitigate these challenges through polygonal abstraction of the underlying mesh, but assume yaw-invariant traversability, rendering them unsuitable for non-circular robots in constrained spaces. We propose SE(2) Navigation Mesh (SE(2) NavMesh), a polygonal representation of traversable regions that encodes yaw-dependent traversability. Our method evaluates traversability using footprint masks and constructs a graph over yaw-specific layers with explicit translational and rotational connectivity. Grounded in this representation, we develop an A*-String Pulling-A* (ASA) pathfinding strategy that hierarchically optimizes robot position and heading. We also present an online method that incrementally updates the SE(2) NavMesh from streaming point clouds during concurrent geometry reconstruction. In simulation, the SE(2) NavMesh captures over 50% more traversable area than classical NavMeshes, and the SE(2) NavMesh + ASA pipeline consistently outperforms sampling-based baselines in constrained environments. Extensive real-world experiments on a physical robot validate real-time online generation and successful navigation across multiple environments.

</details>


### [206] [A Reconfigurable Rocker-Bogie Robot for High Step Climbing and Turning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01554)
*Kento Koizumi,Tomoaki Ohba,Yuta Saito,Takeya Morito,Kenji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构摇臂-转向架机构，通过少量电机实现四轮/六轮切换与零半径转向，同时保持高越障能力。实验表明其转向速度是传统机构的5倍以上，轮扭矩仅为17%，并成功攀爬40cm台阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摇臂-转向架机构使用六个不可转向的抓地轮，转向效率低、扭矩需求大，难以在狭窄空间内高效机动。本研究旨在设计一种兼具高越障能力与高效转向性能的移动机构。

Method: 在转向架关节处安装电机实现主动摆动，使机构在四轮与六轮构型间切换；摇臂后端安装全向轮，在四轮构型下基于差速驱动模型实现平滑转向。

Result: 原型实验表明：零半径转向速度是传统机构的5倍以上，总平均轮扭矩仅为17%；成功攀爬40cm台阶，平均耗时6.4秒。

Conclusion: 所提出的可重构摇臂-转向架机构在保持高越障能力的同时，显著提升了转向性能与能量效率，验证了其在复杂地形下的实用价值。

Abstract: This study proposes a reconfigurable rocker-bogie mechanism that achieves efficient turning motion with a small number of actuators while maintaining high step-climbing capability. By installing motors at the bogie joints and actively swinging up and down bogies, the system enables switching between four-wheel and six-wheel configurations. Omnidirectional wheels are mounted on the rear ends of the rockers, allowing smooth turning in the four-wheel configuration based on a differential-drive model. Experimental evaluation using a prototype robot demonstrated that the proposed mechanism achieves zero-radius turning at a speed more than five times that of a conventional rocker-bogie mechanism equipped with six non-steerable grip wheels, while requiring only approximately 17% of the total average wheel torque. In addition, the robot successfully climbed a 40 cm step with an average climbing time of 6.4 s, confirming its high turning and step-climbing performance.

</details>


### [207] [Multi-Rate Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Wall-Supported Bipedal Locomotion of Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01574)
*Taizoon Chunawala,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多速率非线性模型预测控制（MR-NMPC）的分层规划与控制框架，使四足机器人在受限环境中借助墙壁支撑实现混合双足运动。高层MR-NMPC同时规划接触点（离散）与质心/姿态轨迹（连续），低层全身控制器（WBC）跟踪参考轨迹。仿真验证表明，该方法在不规则地形上的成功率比传统启发式策略高2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在受限环境中执行混合双足运动时，实时轨迹优化面临显著挑战：控制器需同时规划接触点和质心/姿态的连续轨迹，并需处理非线性动力学、单边接触约束、欠驱动以及动力学切换特性。

Method: 提出分层控制框架：高层采用MR-NMPC，基于单刚体动力学模型同时规划离散接触点轨迹和连续质心/姿态轨迹；低层采用基于虚拟约束和二次规划的非线性全身控制器，执行全阶动力学并跟踪MR-NMPC参考。

Result: 在Unitree A1四足机器人上通过数值仿真验证了鲁棒的墙壁辅助双足运动能力，在不规则地形和外部扰动下表现良好。对比分析表明，MR-NMPC在高速穿越不规则地形时的成功率比传统启发式步态规划的MPC高2.9倍。

Conclusion: 将接触点规划集成到多速率最优控制框架中，相比启发式落脚点策略能显著提升动态稳定性，为四足机器人在受限环境中的混合双足运动提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel layered planning and control framework based on multi-rate nonlinear model predictive control (MR-NMPC) that enables quadrupedal robots to perform hybrid bipedal locomotion with wall-assisted support in constrained environments. Real-time trajectory optimization for this locomotion presents significant challenges, as the controller must simultaneously plan for both the contact points and the continuous trajectories of the robot's center of mass (CoM) and orientation within the robot's nonlinear dynamics while accounting for unilateral contact constraints, underactuation, and the switching nature of the robot's dynamics. At the high level of the control framework, an MR-NMPC is proposed, which dynamically plans both the discrete-time trajectories of the contact points and the continuous-time trajectories of the CoM and orientation, using a single rigid body (SRB) dynamics model. By incorporating contact-point planning within the multi-rate optimal control framework, this approach enhances dynamic stability compared to heuristic foot placement strategies. At the low level of the control framework, a nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) based on virtual constraints and a quadratic program enforces full-order dynamics and tracks the MR-NMPC references. The proposed approach is validated through extensive numerical simulations demonstrating the robust wall-assisted bipedal locomotion of a Unitree A1 quadrupedal robot on rough terrains and under external disturbances in a constrained environment. Comparative analysis shows that the proposed MR-NMPC achieves a 2.9 times higher success rate compared to conventional MPC with heuristic-based foot placement strategies in negotiating irregular terrain at high speeds.

</details>


### [208] [Path planning for unmanned naval surface vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01631)
*Daniel G. Schwartz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合全局路径规划器（融合Grassfire、改进Grassfire和新PRM变体）与局部路径规划器（基于障碍物运动方向的高层决策逻辑，引导USV绕到移动障碍物后方避让）的无人水面艇避障新方法，仿真验证了其有效性并与D*算法进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有USV实时避障方法虽多，但针对固定障碍物和移动障碍物（如船只、游泳者等）的避让仍存在挑战，尤其是如何让USV系统性地绕到移动障碍物后方而非平行运行以等待通过机会。

Method: 全局路径规划器组合了三种算法：文献中的Grassfire、一种新的Grassfire改进版，以及一种新型且更直观的概率路线图变体；局部路径规划器基于障碍物相对于USV全局路径的运动方向观察，采用高层决策逻辑决定最佳避让策略，引导USV绕到障碍物后方。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性，并与D*算法及其他动态路径规划系统进行了对比，表明该方法能使USV绕到移动障碍物后方而非平行运行。

Conclusion: 该方法同时解决了固定与移动障碍物避让问题，局部规划器通过绕后策略优于现有系统（如D*），提供了更高效的避障行为。

Abstract: There nowadays is a myriad of approaches to real-time avoidance of fixed obstacles for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) and, to a lesser extent, also the task of avoiding moving obstacles such as boats, ships, swimmers, and other USVs, but both topics still present challenges. This paper offers novel approaches to both of these problems. It uses a combination of a global path planner, which finds a path from a start point to a goal point that avoids fixed obstacles (given that their locations are known in advance), and a local path planner, which can circumnavigate a moving obstacle (as well as any previously unknown fixed obstacles). The global planner is novel in that it employs a combination of three path planners, one known in the literature as Grassfire, one that is a new modification of Grassfire, and one that is a new, and arguably more intuitive, version of the well-known Probabilistic Roadmap. The local planner is novel in that it employs a higher-level decision logic based on its observations regarding the direction of movement of the obstacle relative to the USVs global path. This logic enables the USV to determine the best strategy for avoiding the obstacle by systematically routing the vehicle behind the obstacle rather than running parallel to it until the opportunity to pass appears. Simulations are provided that validate these claims. For comparison with other systems, the simulations include an implementation of the well-known D* algorithm, and the discussion covers additional dynamic path planning systems, which, like D*, do not necessarily route the vehicle behind the moving obstacle.

</details>


### [209] [One Demonstration Is Enough for Real-World Robotic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01651)
*Yuwan Liu,Hongze Yu,Song Liu,Yuhan Wang,Junge Zhang,Yaodong Yang,Yuanpei Chen,Ceyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoSERL利用单次演示通过滑动窗口干预、安全恢复和自动终止机制，在真实世界机器人强化学习中实现完全自动化的干预过程，在六个接触密集型操作任务上优于需20次演示的SERL和单次模仿学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理硬件上学习机器人控制策略面临数据收集成本高和奖励设计困难两大挑战。现有方法要么需要大量演示数据，要么依赖持续的人工干预，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出AutoSERL框架，包含三个互补机制：(1)滑动窗口干预机制，持续引导探索以规避局部最优和安全偏离；(2)安全恢复机制，通过预定义轨迹恢复点检测并纠正失败状态；(3)干预终止准则，在策略能独立完成任务时自动关闭引导，保留探索优势。

Result: 在六个接触密集型操作任务（插入、悬挂、铰链操作）上，AutoSERL一致优于用20次演示初始化的SERL、行为克隆和单次模仿学习基线MILES，匹配HIL-SERL性能，插入任务成功率100%，且对位置变化具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅需单次演示即可在真实世界机器人RL中实现完全自动化干预，显著降低数据需求和人工干预成本，同时保持或超越需要更多演示的对比方法性能。

Abstract: Learning effective robot control policies on physical hardware is challenging due to costly data collection and the difficulty of reward specification. Prior work has incorporated demonstrations into reinforcement learning (RL), yet existing approaches either require large numbers of demonstrations or depend on continuous human intervention during training. To address these limitations, we present AutoSERL, a framework that leverages a single demonstration to fully automate the intervention process in real-world robot RL. The framework includes three complementary mechanisms to accomplish certain tasks: a sliding window intervention mechanism that continuously guides exploration to prevent local optima and unsafe deviations, a safety recovery mechanism that detects and corrects failure states via predefined trajectory recovery points, and an intervention termination criterion that automatically disables guidance once the policy can independently complete the task, preserving its exploration advantage. We evaluate AutoSERL on six contact-intensive manipulation tasks across two robot platforms, spanning insertion, hanging, and hinge-based tasks. AutoSERL consistently outperforms SERL initialized with 20 demonstrations, behavior cloning, and MILES -- a dedicated one-shot imitation learning baseline -- across all tasks while matching HIL-SERL, achieves 100% success rate on insertion tasks, and demonstrates improved robustness to positional variations, all from a single demonstration. Code and videos are available on our project website: https://autoserl.github.io/.

</details>


### [210] [Imagining the Sense of Touch: Touch-Informed Manipulation via Imagined Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01684)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Jyun-Chi Hu,Yosuke Saka,Quan Khanh Luu,Jiuzhou Lei,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Bihao Zhang,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacImag框架，通过从视觉和本体感受预测触觉信号，使机器人在部署时无需触觉传感器也能获得触觉知识，实验证明该方法能显著提升操作性能，且触觉想象的有效性取决于触觉表征与任务需求的匹配程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感在实际部署中面临脆弱性、校准困难和维护负担等限制，因此探索机器人能否在不依赖触觉传感器的情况下利用触觉知识。

Method: 提出TacImag触觉想象框架，从视觉和本体感受预测触觉观测，并用生成的触觉信号引导操作策略；通过配对视觉-触觉演示训练，测试时仅需视觉观测。

Result: 在6个模拟和4个真实任务中验证，想象触觉观测一致提升操作性能；真实实验中，想象力场提升接触敏感任务44.4%，想象触觉图像提升纹理敏感任务23.3%。

Conclusion: 触觉想象不仅仅是对缺失触觉测量的恢复，而是一种接触感知监督形式，能将细微的视觉交互线索转化为更易于操作策略利用的表征。

Abstract: Tactile sensing can substantially improve contact-rich robotic manipulation, yet its practical deployment remains limited by the fragility, calibration requirements, and maintenance burden of tactile hardware. This raises a fundamental question: can robots benefit from tactile knowledge without requiring tactile sensors at deployment? We present TacImag, a tactile imagination framework that predicts tactile observations from vision and proprioception and uses the generated signals to guide manipulation policies. Trained from paired visuotactile demonstrations, TacImag enables touch-informed manipulation using only visual observations at test time. We evaluate TacImag on six simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks. Across simulation and real-world experiments, imagined tactile observations consistently improve manipulation performance without requiring tactile hardware. In real-world experiments, imagined force fields improve contact-sensitive tasks by 44.4% on average, whereas imagined tactile images improve texture-sensitive tasks by 23.3%, revealing that the effectiveness of tactile imagination depends strongly on the relationship between tactile representation and task requirements. Our results further suggest that tactile imagination does not simply recover missing tactile measurements. Instead, it acts as a form of contact-aware supervision that transforms subtle visual interaction cues into representations that are easier for manipulation policies to exploit.

</details>


### [211] [CoRe: Combined Rewards with Vision-Language Model Feedback for Preference-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01721)
*Hexian Ni,Tao Lu,Yinghao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRe提出将奖励分解为形式奖励（FR，基于任务知识显式设计）和残差奖励（RR，从观察中学习隐式偏好），并利用视觉语言模型（VLM）反馈实现无需人工参与的偏好对齐策略学习，在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操控任务中超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计的奖励函数难以指定且可能导致次优策略，而基于偏好的学习奖励存在效率低下和训练不稳定的问题。受认知科学中人类学习的双重性质启发，作者希望将奖励分解为显式的形式奖励和隐式的残差奖励，以兼顾任务知识的明确指导与隐式偏好的灵活学习。

Method: 提出CoRe混合框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 形式奖励模块（FRM），利用VLM基于任务知识和偏好反馈迭代设计和优化形式奖励；(2) 残差奖励模块（RRM），使用VLM生成视频级偏好标签，学习补充FR的残差奖励。FRM与RRM协同工作，实现奖励的自动构建。

Result: 在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操控任务上的实验表明，CoRe在策略学习效果和效率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过形式奖励与残差奖励的协同设计，CoRe实现了高效且与偏好对齐的奖励自动构建，无需人工参与，验证了该框架在机器人操控任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Hand-crafted rewards are often difficult to specify and may lead to suboptimal policies, while learned rewards from preferences can suffer from inefficiency and unstable training. Inspired by the dual nature of human learning explored in cognitive science, we decompose rewards into two complementary components: Formal Rewards (FR), explicitly designed based on task knowledge, and Residual Rewards (RR), learned from observations to capture implicit and nuanced preferences. Based on this decomposition, we propose CoRe, a hybrid framework that integrates FR and RR with vision-language models (VLMs) feedback to achieve preference-aligned policies without human involvement. Our contributions are twofold: (1) We propose a Formal Reward Module (FRM) that leverages VLMs to iteratively design and optimize FR based on task knowledge and preference feedback, enabling the continual improvement of policy during training; (2) We introduce a Residual Reward Module (RRM) that learns RR from video-level preference by employing VLMs to generate preference labels and capturing nuanced rewards that complement FR, ensuring alignment with human intent. Through the synergy of FRM and RRM, CoRe enables the automatic construction of reliable rewards that are efficient and preference-aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoRe outperforms existing approaches in terms of policy learning effectiveness and efficiency on ten robotic manipulation tasks in simulation and five real-worlds. Videos can be found on our project website: https://core-2026.github.io/

</details>


### [212] [Lightweight Safe Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01794)
*Shenghui Zhang,YuXuan Gao,Songwei Zhao,Jifeng Hu,Zijing Zhang,Hechang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机在稀疏感知和动态约束下的自主导航挑战，提出了一种安全约束的感知-控制一体化框架，采用轻量级网络编码稀疏观测为碰撞风险感知特征，基于拉格朗日安全PPO算法在分层控制架构中求解约束马尔可夫决策过程，并通过课程学习提升训练稳定性，在多种障碍密度和飞行速度下实现了更高的成功率、安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法缺乏显式安全机制，导致不安全探索和不稳定训练；安全RL方法通过投影策略到安全动作集可能引入不稳定性；学习型方法依赖密集输入或大网络，计算负担重，难以轻量化部署。

Method: 提出安全约束的感知-控制一体化框架：轻量级网络（非对称+深度可分离卷积）编码稀疏观测为碰撞风险感知特征；将任务建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，在分层控制架构下采用基于拉格朗日的安全PPO算法求解；并引入课程学习提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在多种障碍密度和飞行速度下的实验中，该方法相比现有RL基线实现了更高的成功率、更好的安全性和效率。

Conclusion: 所提安全约束感知-控制一体化框架能有效提升无人机在密集环境中的自主导航性能，兼顾安全性与轻量化部署需求。

Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous aerial systems, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly deployed in applications such as inspection, environmental monitoring, and rescue, creating growing demand for reliable autonomous navigation. However, autonomous UAV navigation in dense environments remains challenging under sparse perception and dynamic constraints. Most reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack explicit safety mechanisms, leading to unsafe exploration, unstable training, and risky behaviors, especially during high-speed flight. Even in safe RL approaches, safety is often enforced by projecting policy outputs onto a safe action set, which may introduce instability. Meanwhile, many learning-based methods rely on dense inputs or large networks, increasing computational burden and limiting lightweight onboard deployment. Facing the above challenges, we propose a safety-constrained perception-control integrated framework for UAV navigation. A lightweight network encodes sparse observations into collision-risk-aware features using asymmetric and depthwise separable convolutions. We formulate the task as a constrained Markov decision process within a hierarchical control architecture and solve it using a Lagrangian-based safe PPO algorithm. Curriculum learning further improves training stability. Experiments with varying obstacle densities and flight speeds demonstrate higher success rates, improved safety, and better efficiency than existing reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [213] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Way of Water框架，将自主水面船队作为分布式编舞平台，通过浏览器端时间线合成编辑器（Way of Water Studio）实现音乐响应式编舞，结合非线性轨迹规划和模型预测控制，并在苏黎世湖和威尼斯双年展进行了实际部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域中的机器人编舞受到非线性流体动力学和环境扰动的严重挑战，现有方法局限于二维水面表现，缺乏面向非编程艺术家的易用编舞工具。

Method: 提出Way of Water Studio，一种浏览器端时间线合成编舞范式，将船队视为类似数字音频工作站的乐器；采用序列凸规划生成轨迹，模型预测控制进行扰动抑制；构建完整信息物理栈（全向底盘、状态估计与控制、LTE/MQTT通信链路、RTK-GPS时间同步）。

Result: 在苏黎世湖的18艘船《天鹅湖》表演和威尼斯双年展Forte Marghera的8艘船演示中验证了系统有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了一个面向流体机器人集群设计与部署的基础参考框架，将高性能水生机器人编舞能力扩展至非程序员艺术家。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [214] [VLA-Corrector: Lightweight Detect-and-Correct Inference for Adaptive Action Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01804)
*Yi Pan,Miao Pan,Qi Lu,Jiaming Huang,Man Zhang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Jie Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Corrector是一种轻量级纠正推理框架，通过潜在空间视觉监控器（LVM）在线检测视觉动态偏差并触发事件驱动的自适应动作截断与重规划，在不修改VLA骨干网络权重的前提下，解决了固定动作分块策略在接触密集型任务中因开环执行累积误差导致任务失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作分块（action chunking）策略采用"先预测后盲目执行"的开环范式，在接触丰富的物理交互中，局部微小扰动会在开环盲区内快速放大，导致累积误差和任务失败。亟需一种既能保留动作分块效率优势，又能恢复闭环反应能力的机制。

Method: 提出VLA-Corrector，包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在空间视觉监控器（LVM），持续比较预测视觉特征与实际视觉特征的演化，在线检测视觉动态偏差；(2) 在线梯度引导（OGG），在检测到持续偏差时触发截断事件，丢弃剩余陈旧动作并调用纠正性重规划。该机制自然形成事件触发的自适应动作时域。

Result: VLA-Corrector能够在不重新训练VLA骨干网络的前提下，集成到不同VLA模型中，中断累积误差的同时保留动作分块的效率优势，显著提升长时域、接触密集型机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLA-Corrector通过事件触发的自适应动作时域缓解了静态时域在执行鲁棒性与策略调用频率之间的权衡，为动作分块VLA策略提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的纠正推理方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently achieved strong progress in embodied intelligence. To reduce policy-call frequency while preserving temporal coherence, most generative policies adopt an action chunk mechanism, executing multiple future actions in an open-loop manner under a fixed action horizon. However, this "predict-then-blindly-execute" paradigm sacrifices closed-loop reactivity: in contact-rich physical interactions, even small local perturbations can rapidly amplify within the open-loop blind spot, leading to compounding errors and ultimately task failure. To address this limitation, we propose VLA-Corrector, a lightweight corrective inference framework for action-chunked VLA policies. Without modifying the backbone policy weights, VLA-Corrector introduces a lightweight Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM) that continuously compares predicted and actual visual feature evolution, enabling online detection of visual dynamics deviations. Once persistent deviation is detected, the system triggers a truncation event, discards the remaining stale actions, and invokes corrective replanning via Online Gradient Guidance (OGG). The detect-and-correct mechanism of VLA-Corrector naturally induces an event-triggered adaptive action horizon: it preserves long-horizon execution when the current chunk remains reliable, and invokes short-horizon corrective replanning when execution begins to drift. In doing so, VLA-Corrector mitigates the trade-off imposed by static horizons between execution robustness and policy-call frequency. It can be integrated into different VLA models without further retraining the VLA backbone, interrupting compounding errors while preserving much of the efficiency benefit of action chunking and substantially improving robustness in long-horizon, contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [215] [DL-SLAM: Enabling High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting SLAM in Dynamic Environments based on Dual-Level Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01860)
*Ziheng Xu,Qingfeng Li,Xuefeng Liu,Chen Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DL-SLAM提出了一种基于双层概率框架的单目高斯泼溅SLAM系统，通过结合语义与几何信息计算动态概率图，并在像素级和物体级进行动态物体处理，从而提升跟踪精度（最高达13%）并生成无伪影的静态地图和高保真语义地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态SLAM方法要么直接丢弃预定义的动态物体，忽略瞬态静态物体对位姿估计的几何约束价值；要么利用逐像素不确定性图来利用瞬态静态物体，但错误地将它们集成到静态地图中导致持久伪影，且仅依赖几何信息导致不确定性图中物体边界模糊。

Method: 提出双层概率框架：1）结合语义和几何信息计算像素级动态概率图；2）将像素级概率提升到3D并聚合得到每个实例的物体级动态概率；3）利用物体级概率对动态高斯体进行分类剪枝，生成无伪影静态地图；4）静态地图提供几何一致性指导来细化像素级概率，形成相互增强的闭环。

Result: 实验表明DL-SLAM优于现有方法，跟踪精度提升高达13%，同时能生成高保真语义地图。

Conclusion: 通过语义与几何信息的融合以及像素级到物体级的双层概率建模，可以有效解决动态场景中瞬态静态物体的利用与静态地图纯净性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in dense dynamic Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). Prevailing methods typically discard predefined dynamic objects, ignoring that transiently static objects offer valuable geometric constraints for pose estimation. A recent work attempts to leverage this potential by employing per-pixel uncertainty maps to quantify the magnitude of motion. While this approach enables transiently static objects to enhance pose estimation, it erroneously integrates these objects into the static map, resulting in persistent artifacts. Moreover, its reliance on purely geometric information leads to ambiguous object boundaries in the uncertainty maps. To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [216] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLC算法，通过社会偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除离线强化学习中为人群机器人导航设计奖励函数的困难，实验证明其在标准指标和真实场景中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在人群机器人导航中潜力巨大，但行人运动的固有复杂性使得设计有效的奖励函数以促进符合社会规范的机器人行为面临持续挑战。

Method: 提出Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC)算法，核心创新在于引入社会偏好反馈机制，通过原则性的偏好评估标准自动生成偏好数据，显式考虑行人动力学复杂性，缓解奖励偏差，系统量化广泛的社会规范。

Result: 与离线RL方法集成的广泛实验表明，SPLC在标准性能指标上一致优于最先进基线；在TurtleBot4上的真实世界实验进一步验证了其在人机共存场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPLC通过社会偏好学习机制有效消除了对详细奖励设计的依赖，能够生成符合社会规范的机器人导航行为，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant potential for crowd robot navigation in human-robot coexistence applications. However, the inherent complexity of pedestrian motion renders the design of effective reward functions for promoting socially compliant robot behaviors a persistent challenge. This paper proposes a Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC) algorithm to eliminate the need for detailed reward design. Its core innovation lies in the introduction of a social preference feedback mechanism to automatically generate preference data through principled preference evaluation criteria. By explicitly accounting for the intricacies of pedestrian dynamics, the pipeline mitigates the reward bias and facilitates the systematic quantification of broad social norms, thereby fostering socially compliant behaviors. Extensive experiments integrating SPLC with offline RL methods demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across standard performance metrics. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the TurtleBot4 further validate the effectiveness of SPLC in practical human-robot coexistence settings. Our code and video demos are available at https://github.com/sklus949/SPLC.

</details>


### [217] [PhysMani: Physics-principled 3D World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01938)
*Peng Yun,Shouwang Huang,Hao Li,Jinxi Li,Jianan Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysMani将物理约束的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知动作策略模型相结合，用于在非结构化3D环境中操控快速移动的物体，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均取得了优于强基线的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型在处理非结构化3D环境中快速动态目标的操控时，缺乏准确的3D几何表示和有物理意义的未来预测能力。

Method: 提出PhysMani框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 物理约束的3D高斯世界模型，通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场，实现快速且物理可信的未来动态预测；(2) 未来感知动作策略模型，通过可学习token的交叉注意力模块整合预测的3D场景未来动态。

Result: 构建了包含16个任务的PhysMani-Bench动态操控基准，在仿真和真实机器人实验中，PhysMani的成功率显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 将物理约束的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知策略相结合，能够有效提升机器人在非结构化环境中操控快速运动物体的能力。

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [218] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCD SLAM是一个动态立体视觉SLAM框架，通过联合处理场景中的动态物体和动态特征，利用"交叉视差"几何方法和3D目标检测+卡尔曼滤波追踪，在KITTI数据集上显著提升了轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM系统在动态环境中由于静态世界假设导致位姿估计和建图失败，需要一种能够鲁棒处理动态场景的SLAM方法。

Method: 提出"交叉视差"几何方法利用时域和立体不一致性检测动态特征点；集成SMOKE 3D目标检测模块与卡尔曼滤波物体追踪进行物体级运动分类；两者互补实现动静场景元素的鲁棒分离。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry和KITTI Raw数据集上，轨迹精度显著优于ORB-SLAM2及多个最先进的动态SLAM方法；消融实验验证了交叉视差模块的有效性，能检测到纯3D目标检测方案遗漏的动态特征。

Conclusion: 交叉视差与3D目标检测的联合方案能有效提升动态环境下的SLAM鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: This paper presents OCD SLAM, a dynamic stereo visual SLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM2 by jointly addressing dynamic objects and dynamic features in the scene. Usual visual SLAM systems operating in dynamic environments often fail in the presence of moving objects, due to the static-world assumption used in pose estimation and mapping. To address this predicament, we introduce a novel geometric approach based on the discrepancy between disparity and a newly proposed notion called ``cross disparity'', which exploits both temporal and stereo inconsistency to identify dynamic feature points. Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [219] [Cross-Platform Control for Autonomous Surface Vehicles via Adaptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02037)
*Ruiheng Jiang,Thomas Bi,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应强化学习方法，通过教师-学生架构推断平台动力学的潜在表征，实现水面航行器轨迹跟踪策略的零样本跨平台部署，在真实实验中相比非自适应基线最高降低58%的位置误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面航行器的水动力与驱动特性差异巨大，但现有控制器多为单一平台设计，缺乏跨平台泛化的能力。

Method: 采用部分可观测性框架，通过交互历史条件化；使用教师-学生架构学习平台动力学的潜在表征；在随机化船体动力学的仿真中训练策略，零样本部署到真实平台。

Result: 在两个不同真实平台上，自适应策略相比非自适应学习基线在位置平均绝对误差上降低高达58%，接近平台专用调优控制器的跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 该方法仅依赖简单的解析动力学模型而非高保真水动力仿真器，即可实现有效的零样本跨平台迁移，展示了在多样化水面航行器上的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles vary widely in hydrodynamic and actuation characteristics, yet most controllers are designed for single-platform deployment. We present an adaptive reinforcement learning approach for trajectory tracking that enables zero-shot cross-platform deployment using a single policy. Since the deployment platform's dynamics are unknown to the policy, we address cross-platform generalization with the standard partial-observability approach of conditioning on interaction history, employing a teacher-student architecture in which a learned module infers a latent representation of the platform dynamics. The policy is trained in simulation under randomized vessel dynamics and is deployed zero-shot to two real-world platforms without any fine-tuning, despite relying on a simple analytical dynamics model rather than a high-fidelity hydrodynamic simulator. In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>
